UORT development input is 85776 physical bytes = 64 header bytes + 42856 decoded tokens; mask 42856, eligible 14287 targets across 400 documents, 111 global 384-token blocks + 232-token tail. Tokenizer SHA256 `d36d3e8700a123e620012df77de195f244fbdb4d05d9e1aa7e77fa9407590f89` is recovered from private-store commit `435d5f781cd7b8518185cf6e7fda2a7addb0532a` with SHA/byte round-trip verification. **Latest geometric head/shared-writer delivery — training remains held.**

Protected [PR #2301](https://github.com/UOR-Foundation/uor-r4/pull/2301) merged at

`head=spiralcore_product` requires `projection=spiralcore_fbs`: token input,
raw-distance output and compiler/exact-store/emitter vector adapters borrow the
**same learned embedding Tensor/Var**. Fixed SpiralCore product ports admit
bounded exact-identity cells; identity modulo 256 is address metadata, not a
semantic metric. Typed candidate IDs/masks preserve collisions and padding;
raw scores are negative mean-L1 displacement at fixed scale 1, with smallest-ID
ties. Default 8 cells gives 128 comparisons at V=4096, bounded above by 512. A full-V
compatibility score allocation remains. Omitted targets receive explicit score
credit without injection into inference candidates; separate token/address
margins have weight 1 each, with address credit declared an auxiliary surrogate.
The old whole-path probability artifact admission, choice, exponential table
pool and probability loss refuse; removed source is indexed/restorable.

Five finite tests PASS in 1.45s (static binding/CPU refusal plus four CUDA
controls), including actual Stack/store/channel/artifact connections, FBS
transport, distance adjoints, omitted-target/address credit, exact ties/padding
and numerical/device boundaries. CUDA test compilation PASS 10.76s; CLI compile
PASS 3.83s. Review corrected three concrete API defects: host-score trace helper,
mutable carried identity and reverse-order pointer/public-config validation.
Kimi's recurrence #2293 is integrated but is not device-qualified by this unit.

### Acceptance (PROPOSED — owner to confirm)
1. Saved model completes **≥ 256/512** correct replies on the frozen development panel.
2. Then **≥ 40%** on a fresh held-out draw with criteria frozen before the draw.
3. Gains must come from the saved artifact under its own prefix, not from a probe or a selector-only repair.

### How to work on this issue
- Claim with one comment: lab, goal, expected cost; add your `lab:*` label and set `status:in-progress`.
- Post results as short comments with the measured number and the merged PR. No heartbeats or lease renewals here: those go to the compute board.
- Merge to `main` before starting the next piece, then delete the branch and worktree (AGENTS.md, 9 October rule).
- This issue closes only when its acceptance is met on the saved model. A negative result is recorded here and the issue stays open.

# Saved437 Geometric Carrier Connection

Status: prospective; no model compute authorized by this document alone. External TEST FITNESS is required on #2030, and source/device gates must pass before the first optimizer update.

## Causal Question

Does training the actual factual/local H4 carriers and current Generate prototypes through the retained ordered cross-state field improve complete own-prefix answers and transfer? This is a missing learning connection in an already finite geometric M2 path, not a new transformer, selector, rate sweep, acoustic encoding or universal SpiralCore conversion claim.

The implementation uses independent full120 conditional utilities with other carriers fixed at their current hard choices. It preserves exact hard forward scores and the selected coefficient STE once. It does not compute a joint posterior or claim to differentiate the hard categorical choice.

## Base And Conditions

- Source preparation: commits `927c435cc` and `02688fd31`, plus a final exact-head runner/source qualification receipt before launch. Later source repairs are setup corrections, recorded explicitly; scientific changes require renewed registration/FIT.
- Saved report SHA256: `787f1a694dd894f8dcf636e158b2470fd88a824f3dd73535749bc4f36f05a165`; seal `2f630ad56a6abd09271681fcb514d22caefac8af918321224bc173446c20a84c`.
- Saved native field `de4a3234d6e92f4b12a657cc195425687bc67c243d9eded9a1df0797b9942a61`; exact fractional field master `af5e263c2e3dca56232ba7d43f52514c0a4fbfd5c79956df0cfa0ebe1567fc88`. The latter is the continuation master, not a Generate master.
- Both arms start independently from the identical Source48/Generate64 upstream, saved437 v3 field, and original fractional masters. No optimizer state is imported. No LP replay is duplicated or resumed.
- Active parameters: the nine named shared Context transition/root/category tensors selected by `context_state_parameter`, and `generate.prototype_choices`. No new trainable parameter or target-dependent route.
- Frozen: Potential, Generate energy and bias, original and categorical bridges, Cue, Prefix, continuation numeric masters, tokenizer, legal vocabulary, all source records/versions, grader and panels. Native sidecar binding metadata is refreshed after updates; numeric frozen masters are hash-checked.
- All4096 Generate tokens and every physical Copy alias enter the same existing clip/pool. EOS is a trained and evaluated action. Candidate admission is not changed. Exact IDs are not semantic distances, and a geometric prototype is not an occurrence identity.

## Matched Arms

For seeds1001 and2001, run carrier-credit ON and OFF, four sealed independent reports. Both arms learn the same active parameters and retain ordinary Generate/Copy credit; OFF disables only the new v3 field-to-carrier/prototype adjoints. No cached hard state survives an update.

Two epochs of the same exposed512 rows: batch8,128 updates, one pinned seed-specific Fisher-Yates order cycled twice. All answer positions including EOS, equal-episode logmeanexp of the existing strongest-wrong pooled negative-log-mass margin0 softplus. No phase reweighting, target-fed factual state, row subsampling or checkpoint selection.

Context AdamW rate0.002; prototype rate0.01; beta0.9/0.999, epsilon1e-8, decay0. One global gradient clip1 over the same active inventory; Context masters clipped to [-1.75,1.75], prototypes unclipped. Checkpoints32/64/96/128 are recovery artifacts, not a selection sweep. Only128 is scored as the endpoint. These fixed rates implement the connection test; changing rate/dose/seed is not a decisive successor mechanism test.

## Gates And Measurements

1. Host arithmetic/conditional-credit/config fixtures and one explicit authenticated saved-artifact position. Missing artifacts must fail, never conditionally pass. These are source gates, not model measurements.
2. Actual CUDA build/kernel execution and original-eight portable/native whole-pool parity, including Copy raw scores and EOS. Tooling/numerical failure is a repair blocker, never a model negative.
3. Before the first update of each arm, all512 baseline own-prefix sequences/completions must match saved437 exactly, along with each canonical factual/local state, Generate/U score digest, physical Copy IDs and pool summary. Saved rows do not contain a Copy-raw digest; do not claim that unavailable full512 bit comparison. Baseline must remain437/512.
4. Independent endpoint export/master/native reload, frozen-master checks, all512 own-prefix generation and rowwise changes against the saved base and matched OFF seed. Native teacher-prefix CE is separate from complete replies.
5. Seal all four development reports before evaluating the existing opened128 inputs and labels. Evaluate all four endpoints on that identical diagnostic draw, reporting gains/losses, EOS and per-bank counts. No redraw, answer changes or score-guided endpoint selection. This is opened diagnosis, not genuinely fresh qualification.
6. If a candidate advances, choose ON endpoint by the preregistered rule: larger development complete count, tie lower native equal-episode CE, tie seed1001. Only then perform the later genuinely fresh qualification under the existing owner acceptance protocol, independently frozen before its new draw. No claim of M2 acceptance or D22 fresh-transfer KEEP until that qualification exists.

The exact factor kernel's measured fixture count reduction (32768->960 coefficient reads and65536->1920 H4 products) is retained independently of model quality. Full4096 token/prototype reads remain. Record real device/host time, transfers, peak memory and wall cost; no whole-model speed or energy claim from operation counts. CUDA long training/evaluation is mandatory, but host artifact preparation and alias reduction remain explicit.

## Bars And Opposite Decisions

Practical advancement requires BOTH ON seeds >=461/512, no loss greater than24 saved-success rows, and >=12/128 diagnostic completions across >=4/8 banks in each replicate. Report the two matched OFF outcomes and paired row changes; ON must also beat its matched OFF by >=24 complete development rows in both seeds to attribute practical improvement to the new connection. The24-row margin is a prospective conservative bar, not an estimated confidence interval or a claim that panel noise was measured.

- If the replicated bar and matched contrast pass, retain the selected endpoint and proceed to genuinely fresh qualification. Even then, exposed/diagnostic success alone is panel fitting, not M2 acceptance.
- If both arms improve similarly but their contrast fails, preserve useful candidates without attributing improvement to field carrier credit. The next causal task concerns the shared Context/prototype adaptation, not a new ON seed/rate sweep.
- If ON loses >24 saved successes, or the hard choices barely move despite finite nonzero surrogate gradients, preserve the configuration result and investigate credit/hard-choice alignment or the representation boundary before another scientific run. Do not close H4, SpiralCore or unified geometry as mechanisms.
- If ON helps development but not the opened128 diagnostic, preserve the result explicitly as panel fitting and advance a model-changing generalization/representation connection. Do not tune on that diagnostic draw.
- A setup failure repairs the same causal test. A novel-mechanism stop requires the owner. No inference of general chat, frontier reasoning or full-path energy savings is made.

M2 acceptance remains saved own-prefix >=256/512 AND genuinely fresh >=40%, merged result PR/final card. No issue closure is permitted from this test alone.
